Unruh's South Russian Mennonite Census, 1795-1814 (A. Peters)

        Published by the Odessa Digital Library - 13 May 1995
             http://www.odessa3.org

               Index to Mennonites in B. H. Unruh

This index lists names, estimated birth dates, village names, and the
source page number for Mennonites who lived in the Black Sea region of
Russia. Most of the original sources are revisions (census) lists dated
from 1795 to 1814.  All of these lists are difficult to read and some of
them can no longer be located. The compilation was published in 1955 by
Professor Benjamin Unruh in a book entitled Die
niederlaendisch-niederdeutschen Hintergruende der mennonitischen
Ostwanderungen in 16., 18., und 19. Jahrhundert.  Most of the entries in
the book describe family groups; and some entries provide a short inventory
of possessions.  The index in Unruh's book is far from comprehensive and
does not list birth dates.  Copies of Unruh's book are available at
Mennonite archives (North Newton, Kansas; Hillsboro, Kansas; Fresno,
California; and Winnipeg, Manitoba, Canada), some university libraries, and
on film from LDS Family History Libraries.

The index below was compiled by Alan Peters in 1991.  Most of the column
headings are self-explanatory.  The colonies are abbreviated: Chor for
Chortitza and Mol for Molotschna.  Maiden? indicates whether or not a
female surname is the woman's maiden name.  Code provides a number that
links equivalent surnames that have variations in spelling, e.g. Ahrend,
Arndt, Arend.
